[[smc-vplan]]
== SMC OCAH Open-Source Verification Plan

=== Overview

This plan covers the SMC subsystem as it is verified in the open-source
harness: the `smc_wrapper` DUT driven over SEP_IN AXI and AXI-Lite, with
cocotb/PyUVM as the primary framework and an SV-UVM view for the scenarios
that carry one. The enrolled testcases and the membership of the `all`
sign-off group are defined in `testlists/all.toml`. The seven enrolled
leaves outside `all`, and why, are named in the `all` row under
Regression Groups; where the reason is a defect, Known Limitations records
the hole.

Every entry below names a testcase that exists, and every card says which
groups enrol it; a card whose groups no scheduled tier selects says so and
records no pass. Where the harness
cannot reach a feature, the testcase is not listed here as enrolled; it is
recorded under Known Limitations with the reason, so this plan states what
is verified rather than what was once intended.

=== Verification Strategy

* *Frontdoor stimulus, frontdoor proof.* Stimulus is driven through product
  ports -- SEP_IN AXI, AXI-Lite, JTAG, and the pad-level protocol VIPs -- and
  every claim is proven by what the DUT returns or drives at those ports and
  on its own outputs. Where the DUT offers no frontdoor way to create the
  condition a testcase verifies -- a corrupted ECC codeword in a scratch
  macro, an unused encoding in a state flop, an error from the eFuse bank
  model -- the testcase may create it with a testbench hook: a backdoor write
  into the macro array, a force on the flop, or an inject pin on the bound DV
  memory collateral. Such a card names the hook on its *BFM / Model* line and
  in its Test Procedure, and the DUT's reaction is still observed frontdoor,
  on the DUT's outputs and registers, never on the poked or forced signal
  itself. Passive hierarchical taps read state and never write it. The rule
  holds for the `all` sign-off sweep and for every card that claims sign-off
  PASS; a leaf whose checks still read the forced signal is held out of `all`
  and recorded under Known Limitations until its checks move to the registers.
* *Checked, not observed.* A read that is printed but not compared is not
  evidence. Expectations come from the generated RDL reset values, the
  preloaded assets, or the specification -- never from a value the DUT
  returned earlier in the same run.
* *Negative checks carry a positive control.* A claim that a signal stays
  low is only meaningful if the same run shows it going high.
* *Evidence tokens.* Each testcase emits `CHK-*` lines that name what was
  compared and against what, so a kept log can be read without the source.

=== Naming Rules

* Testcases are `smc_<feature>_<scenario>_test`, lower snake case.
* The cocotb module and the testlist entry share that name.
* Sequences are `<testcase>_seq`, in `cocotb/seq_lib/`.
* Evidence tokens are `CHK-<AREA>-<CLAIM>`, upper kebab case.

=== Open-Source BFM Policy

Shared and vendored components are used where they exist. A local model is
written only where none fits, and every one carries a README beside it
naming what it replaces and why -- see `dv/models/README.md`.

Bus terminators answer a port that a real integration fills with the
adopter's CSRs. They are declared as terminators, never presented as
verifying the block behind the port.

==== OCCP

Two-instance and production-ROM scenarios. These build a different DUT from the
rest of the plan -- `target = "dual"` elaborates `SMC_DUAL`, and the OCCP family
runs `hw/sys/smc/bootrom/prod` instead of a DV stub image -- so they are
enrolled through `dual_*` and `occp_*` rather than `all`.

[cols="2,3",options="header",]
|===
|Testcase |Objective
|`smc_dual_elaboration_test` |Prove the two-instance model elaborates and both instances leave reset, sense their fuses and finish memory init from the shared cold reset.
|`smc_dual_axi_sram_probe_test` |Measure that an inbound AXI manager reaches the CPU scratch SRAM window.
|`smc_occp_sanity_test` |One SMC issues OCCP GET_VERSION at another and reports through an OCCP WRITE.
|`smc_occp_dual_unsecure_boot_test` |OCCP unsecure boot end to end between two real SMCs over the shared I3C bus.
|`smc_prod_rom_occp_ready_test` |Production boot ROM reaches its OCCP command processing loop.
|===

==== RTL ISSUE REPRODUCERS

Tests whose job is to fail. Each holds the runnable evidence for one RTL
defect, so the defect has a measurement rather than a description alone. All
are enrolled through `rtl_issue` -- no `ci` tag, not in `smoke`, `hosted` or
`all`. Every `[[tests]]` entry carries `expect_fail` naming the issue and
`expect_fail_match` pinned to the assertion it raises; the weekly tier runs
the group on its own `regress.yml` row and grades the recorded failure as
PASS, so the row goes red (`expected_fail_passed`) the week a defect is
fixed and the leaf moves to its feature testlist. The members are
`smc_deadspace_decode_test` (#585) and `smc_remap_cla_test` (#1855), whose
cards are under FABRIC, and `smc_efuse_map_read_test` (#1989), whose card is
under EFUSE.

Every one carries an arm leg that must pass before its defect leg is scored.
Without that, a stuck-at field or a dead probe would satisfy the defect leg for
the wrong reason.

==== FIRMWARE

[cols="2,3",options="header",]
|===
|Testcase |Objective
|`smc_fw_hello_world_test` |Boot the smallest SMC C image and read the verdict it posts over MMIO.
|`smc_fw_hello_world_multicore_test` |All four harts of the CPU cluster run the scratch image and check in with each other; `smc_fw_hello_world_test` proves hart 0 only.
|`smc_fw_octs_p0_primary_test` |The OCTS system timer is configured and swept from the hart that owns it in the product: CTRL writability, TIMER_GPIO_ENABLE, 64-bit PRESET, TIMER_START, monotonic COUNT.
|`smc_fw_i2c_sanity_test` |I2C controller bring-up from the CPU, the `i2c_sanity` image: TIMING0-4 read-back, FMT reset on a non-empty FIFO, a 3-byte write with STOP received by the I2C_1 target, the CMD_COMPLETE lifecycle through the PLIC pending bit, CONTROLLER_HALT provoked by a NACK.
|`smc_fw_i2c_target_sanity_test` |I2C target bring-up from the CPU, the `i2c_target_sanity` image: ADDR0 match and mismatch, dual address, ACQ write, TX read, TX stretch control -- and the UNEXP_STOP lifecycle with the bench as the external host.
|`smc_fw_i2c_acq_fifo_stretch_reset_test` |The target's ACQ FIFO stretches SCL automatically when full, and ACQ reset releases the stretch, observed on the target's own SCL driver.
|`smc_fw_i2c_p1_fifo_depth_xfer_test` |A full-FIFO-depth 64-byte transfer with the controller pushing while the target drains; length and every byte compared, NACKed entries excluded from the payload.
|`smc_fw_i2c_tx_stretch_timeout_recovery_test` |The controller's stretch timeout fires on a read the empty target TX FIFO stretches; the recovery -- target disable releases SCL, controller disable drives the automatic STOP, both re-initialised -- ends in a clean 16-byte write.
|`smc_fw_i2c_fifo_full_test` |Fill and reset of the four FIFOs of an I2C pair: FMT and TX by software stores to the declared depth, RX and ACQ by real 8-byte transfers, with the driver's software-drain repair flag read back so a repaired FIFO fails.
|`smc_fw_uart_log_engine_single_entry_test` |Log-engine golden path: one 16-byte SRAM entry fetched and written to UART0, returned through MCR.LOOP and read back from RBR in order; LOG_CTRL[0] hwclr; INTR_STATUS 0.
|`smc_fw_uart_log_engine_boundary_backpressure_test` |Log-engine effective-length boundaries -- request shorter than the slot, request clamped to the slot, non-word-aligned slot rounded to one beat -- and a log write under UART TX backpressure.
|`smc_fw_uart_log_engine_disable_during_xfer_test` |CTRL.EN cleared mid-transfer halts the engine, measured as fewer bytes than the entry reaching the UART and none after it goes idle; a re-trigger delivers exactly its bytes; four entries triggered together all hwclr; the same halt measurement on an abort from WAIT; replica 1 runs an entry.
|`smc_fw_uart_log_engine_fetch_err_test` |A log-engine fetch from an unmapped address latches INTR_STATUS.LOG_FETCH_ERR: INTR_TEST self-test, INTR_ENABLE gating, stuck-cause re-latch and retirement by a good fetch, sustained DECERR with LOG_CTRL not hwclr, WRITE_ERR staying clear, and the fault on replica 1.
|===

=== Test Procedures

==== MISC

[[sec-001-smc_5agent_observability_test]]
===== 1 `smc_5agent_observability_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_5agent_observability_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: SMC OSS PyUVM 5-agent full observability test.

*Test Coverage*:

* A bare idle IRQ sample, whose `sync_irq` / `gpio_irq_any` / `uart_irq_any` == 0 compares also pass on a stuck-at-0 or mis-bound probe, proves nothing on its own. The SAMPLE is therefore preceded by a **positive control**: GPIO0 is armed over the real SEP_IN AXI frontdoor and driven low at the pad until `tb_gpio_irq_any` is observed at 1, and an IRQ item carrying `expect_gpio_irq_any = 1` is sampled inside that window (`[NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]`).
* A single SAMPLE gated only on `item.resolvable` gives `CHK-5AGENT-GPIO` no FAIL-ON path, because `resolvable` cannot be False under the 2-state simulator every retained run uses. The GPIO leg is therefore a *pair* of SAMPLEs whose expectation is stated on the **raw pad-bus vectors** (`tb_core2pad_o` / `tb_core2pad_en_o`), backed in the same run by `ensure_gpio_pad_bus_control`. The three `tb_gpio_*_any` aggregates are unbackable in this TB and stay OBSERVED-ONLY -- the scoreboard refuses a stated expectation on them (`[EVIDENCE-TOKEN-CONDITIONAL]`, `[NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]`).
* The 5-agent composition claim is gated on the **per-type** scoreboard counters, not on the aggregate `total > 0` gate, so a missing item type is a composition failure instead of an invisible hole (`[EXACT-EXPECTATION]`).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-5AGENT-CLK` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-COMPOSITION` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-GPIO` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-I2C` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-IDLE` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-POSITIVE` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-5AGENT-RESET` |Named compare in `smc_5agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-5AGENT-CLK`, `CHK-5AGENT-COMPOSITION`, `CHK-5AGENT-GPIO`, `CHK-5AGENT-I2C`, `CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-IDLE`, `CHK-5AGENT-IRQ-POSITIVE` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `combined`, `pyuvm`.
'''''

[[sec-002-smc_6agent_observability_test]]
===== 2 `smc_6agent_observability_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_6agent_observability_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite)

*Objective*: SMC OSS PyUVM 6-agent full observability test.

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequence for smc_6agent_observability_test.
* All six agents (reset / i2c / clk / irq / gpio / axil) sampled in one scenario.
* The ultimate full-env demonstration.
* Each agent's mapped expectation is asserted here and only then does the body emit that agent's `CHK-6AGENT-<AGENT>:` evidence token, so no token can be printed from the setup path or from an item whose compare did not run (`[EVIDENCE-TOKEN-CONDITIONAL]`).
* The composition claim is gated on the **scoreboard's typed counters** (`EXPECTED_SAMPLES`), which are incremented on the analysis path: a mis-bound agent, a dropped item type or a monitor that never published fails the gate.
* The ordered `chk_seen` list is kept only as a readable record of which legs ran -- it is appended by `_chk` itself in the same straight-line body, so comparing it against a literal could not fail on any RTL, so it is not the composition check (`[NO-ALWAYS-PASS-CHECKER]`).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-6AGENT-AXIL` |AXIL compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-CLK` |CLK compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-COMPOSITION` |Named compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-6AGENT-GPIO` |GPIO compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-I2C` |I2C compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-IRQ` |IRQ compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-6AGENT-RESET` |RESET compare in `smc_6agent_observability_test_seq.py`
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-6AGENT-AXIL`, `CHK-6AGENT-CLK`, `CHK-6AGENT-COMPOSITION`, `CHK-6AGENT-GPIO`, `CHK-6AGENT-I2C`, `CHK-6AGENT-IRQ` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `combined`, `pyuvm`.
'''''

[[sec-003-smc_canonical_smoke_test]]
===== 3 `smc_canonical_smoke_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_canonical_smoke_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI-Lite)

*Objective*: Run six-agent observability across the reset recovery matrix.

*Test Coverage*:

* Canonical high-density SMC smoke sequence.
* This sequence combines the six-agent observability smoke with the reset recovery matrix in one canonical test.
* Synchronization contract: no step waits a magic number of `clk_ref_i` cycles and then samples.
* Every one is a bounded poll on the real reset observables via `SmcResetOp.WAIT_STATE` -- the driver polls until the exact expected state appears and the scoreboard converts bound expiry into a testcase failure with the last observed state (`[NO-BLIND-DELAY-SYNC]`, `[TIMEOUT-MUST-FAIL]`).
* Mid-window `RAW_SAMPLE` snapshots carry the exact asserted expectation they are taken for, so they are checked evidence instead of observations (`[EXACT-EXPECTATION]`).
* The remaining fixed `ClockCycles` are *driven stimulus widths* (how long a pin is held low), which is the quantity the sequence owns, never a stand-in for a completion handshake.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CANONICAL-SMOKE` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_PRIMARY_ASSERTED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_RELEASED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_PRIMARY_ASSERTED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_RELEASED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-PG_ASSERTED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-RESET-WAIT-PG_RELEASED` |Named compare in `smc_canonical_smoke_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-CANONICAL-SMOKE`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_PRIMARY_ASSERTED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COLD_RELEASED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_PRIMARY_ASSERTED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-COOL_RELEASED`, `CHK-RESET-WAIT-PG_ASSERTED` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `combined`, `pyuvm`, `smoke`.
'''''

[[sec-005-smc_default_reg_rd_test]]
===== 5 `smc_default_reg_rd_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_default_reg_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Run a compact OSS-safe default-register read sweep.

*Test Coverage*:

* Default register read smoke over the real SEP_IN AXI ingress port.
* The sweep reads side-effect-free internal SMC CSRs through `sep_axi_in_req_i` and checks that every selected address returns an OKAY AXI response **and** — wherever the generated register map defines one — the exact reset value for that register.
* Access-port identity: the test starts this sequence on `env.sys_axi_agent`, whose driver declares `bus_prefix = "s_axi"` / `bus_name = "SEP_IN AXI"` (`env/smc_sys_axi_agent.py`), and `tb_top.sv` wires the top-level `s_axi_*` pins into `smc.sep_axi_in_req_i`.
* The SYS_IN port (`sys_axi_*` -> `sys_axi_in_req_i`) is driven by the separate `env.sys_in_axi_agent`, which this test never starts; despite its name, `env.sys_axi_agent` drives SEP_IN.
* What that does and does not prove: every register read below lives inside `smc_misc_wrap` and is reached over the same internal register fabric from either ingress port, so the reset-default proof holds regardless of which port carried it.
* It gives NO coverage of the SYS_IN ingress port itself.
* Addresses come from the generated PeakRDL map only (indexed `SCRATCH_BASE_ADDR(idx)` macros and per-register `*_BASE_ADDR` symbols in `hw/sys/smc/regs/gen/c/smc_addr.h`); expected values come from `smc_csr_field_catalog` so the address and the value share one source.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DEFAULT-REG-SWEEP` |Named compare in `smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE` |Named compare in `smc_default_reg_rd_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE-COMPARE-FLOOR` |Named compare in `smc_default_reg_rd_test.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-DEFAULT-REG-SWEEP`, `CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE`, `CHK-DEFAULT-REG-VALUE-COMPARE-FLOOR` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `functional_depth`, `pyuvm`, `depth`.
'''''

[[sec-007-smc_fw_plic_claim_test]]
===== 7 `smc_fw_plic_claim_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_fw_plic_claim_test`

*BFM / Model*: the SMC C image `fw/tests/plic_sanity`, executed by the CPU cluster; stimulus on `tb_ext_interrupt_0_i`, verdict read over SEP_IN AXI

*Objective*: Firmware programs the cluster PLIC and claims a real external interrupt through it.

*Test Coverage*:

* The cluster PLIC's enable, priority, threshold and claim-complete path, which **no cocotb sequence in this package can reach**. The 0xC400_0000 aperture answers SEP_IN AXI reads but takes no writes from it -- Known Limitations records that measurement. The CPU is the only master that can program it.
* The loop `smc_ext_interrupts_pin_test` states in its own docstring that it leaves open: that testcase follows `ext_interrupts_i[0]` through `prim_sync3` and "does not claim PLIC". `smc_hang_detector_plic_route_test` checks a route with testbench probes and reads no PLIC register either. This closes it end to end: pin -> synchroniser -> gateway -> enable and priority against threshold -> MEIP -> trap -> claim -> the ID the CPU reads back -> complete.
* PLIC source 1, which is `cpu_interrupts[0]` and therefore `ext_interrupts_i[0]`: source IDs are the line index plus one because ID 0 is reserved, and `smc_base.sv:249` puts `ext_interrupts` at the bottom of the vector. It is source 1 and not another because `tb_top.sv:1199-1200` drives only `ext_interrupts_i[1:0]` -- bit 0 from `tb_ext_interrupt_0_i`, bit 1 from `tb_temp_interrupt_i` -- and ties `[255:2]` to zero, and this testcase raises bit 0 alone: any other external source is a line nothing raises here, so the handler would never run and the testcase would time out instead of measuring anything.
* No forced internal state: the stimulus is a DUT input pin and everything between it and the verdict is the DUT ([NO-FORCED-INTERNAL-STATE]).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Build `plic_sanity` through `[c_build.default]`, stage `plic_sanity.ecc.hex`, hold `boot_stall` from t=0, clear CPU_CTRL SCRATCH_0 and read the clear back.
. Program the reset vector and release. The image clears every context's enable words, gives the eight contexts distinct thresholds, registers a handler for source 1, enables it, publishes the arm word 0xAAAA_AAAA and parks in `wfi`. The handler masks the source through the context threshold (not the enable bit, which the PLIC requires set for a completion to count) and returns, so the driver's claim-complete write executes; main then lowers the threshold with the pin still high and requires a second delivery before it posts the verdict, because the gateway forwards a source again only after its previous claim was completed.
. Require the arm word before any verdict. A PASS not preceded by it is failed: the image would have reached its verdict without passing through the state the stimulus acts on.
. Hold the pin low for 2000 further `clk_smc_i` cycles and require SCRATCH_0 to still read the arm word.
. Drive `ext_interrupts_i[0]` high and poll for the verdict.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-FW-PLIC-QUIET` |With the PLIC armed and the pin still low, SCRATCH_0 does not move. Without this the PASS could have come from any interrupt already pending, and the testcase would be satisfied by a DUT that traps on something else
|`CHK-FW-PLIC-STIMULUS` |The pin rises only after the quiet window, so the verdict below is ordered after the stimulus rather than merely coincident with it
|`CHK-FW-PLIC-CLAIM` |The firmware's own PASS word reaches CPU_CTRL SCRATCH_0, and only two completed claims of the registered source can put it there: the verdict is posted after the source, unmasked with the pin still high, is delivered a second time, which the PLIC gateway does only once the first claim has been completed. `riscv_plic0.c:106-107` dispatches `metal_exint_table[idx]` on the index the claim register returned, and the image registers a handler at one index only -- every other source reaches `__metal_plic0_default_handler`, which posts no verdict. The handler's own ID compare is unreachable for that reason and is not credited here
|===

*Pass Criteria*: all three checkers hold. The verdict poll is bounded at 6000 iterations of 100 `clk_smc_i` cycles; the image spends most of the boot in `__metal_driver_riscv_plic0_init`, which walks all 336 sources over MMIO. No `ci` tag: the hosted runners have no RISC-V toolchain. Enrolled in: `all`, `fw`.

'''''

[[sec-009-smc_register_sanity_test]]
===== 9 `smc_register_sanity_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_register_sanity_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Run the SMC OSS register-sanity scenario.

*Test Coverage*:

* read reset value from SMC scratch registers,
* write distinct patterns,
* read back the exact values.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_register_sanity_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CSR-IDLE-VALUE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): idle read of each scratch register is zero after warm-reset release
|`CHK-CSR-RANDOM-READBACK` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): each seeded random pattern reads back on the same register
|`CHK-CSR-READBACK` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): directed write pattern reads back on the same register
|`CHK-CSR-RESTORE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): restore write of zero reads back
|`CHK-CSR-RESP` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): every SEP_IN CSR access completes with an OKAY response
|`CHK-FUSE-SENSE-DONE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): warm-reset domain release after fuse sense completes
|`CHK-NONVAC` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): non-vacuity count of real SEP_IN CSR accesses in `smc_register_sanity_test_seq.svh`
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-CSR-IDLE-VALUE`, `CHK-CSR-RANDOM-READBACK`, `CHK-CSR-READBACK`, `CHK-CSR-RESP`, `CHK-CSR-RESTORE`, `CHK-FUSE-SENSE-DONE` (and 1 more) hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `functional`, `functional_depth`, `pyuvm`, `smoke`, `depth`.
'''''

==== POWER

[[sec-010-smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test]]
===== 10 `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: AVS_CFG answers with AVS_CG_EN cleared and stops answering when set.

*Test Coverage*:

* **Positive control** -- with the gate CLEARED, all three AVS_CFG windows must COMPLETE, and their real round-trip latency is *measured* here rather than assumed ([NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]).
* **Negative leg** -- with the gate SET, the same three reads must fail to answer within a bound DERIVED from that measurement (`_GATED_BOUND_FACTOR` x the slowest healthy round-trip, floored at `_MIN_GATED_BOUND_NS`). A fixed bound shorter than a healthy CSR round-trip would hold on a fully awake AVSBus -- it would measure latency, not decode ([NO-ALWAYS-PASS-CHECKER]) -- which is why the bound is measured, and why the same-bound ungated control below shows a responsive window satisfies it.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-AVSBUS-CG` |Named compare in `smc_avsbus_clock_config_proxy_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-SIDEBAND-OBSERVABILITY` |Named compare in `smc_sideband_vip_utils.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-AVSBUS-CG`, `CHK-SIDEBAND-OBSERVABILITY` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `avsbus_depth`, `per_ip`, `functional_depth`.
'''''

[[sec-013-smc_dma_sanity_test]]
===== 13 `smc_dma_sanity_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_dma_sanity_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_jtag_vip

*Objective*: Run DMA copy over output-fabric payload bytes and check the model.

*Test Coverage*:

* DMA source-to-destination payload check through the output-fabric responder.
* A second descriptor with LENGTH = 1 separates "the programmed length was honoured" from "a whole 64-bit beat was copied": the destination word's upper seven bytes must still read the poison this leg wrote.
* The SYS_OUT responder is shared, and the pass-all outbound filter this sequence programs lets other masters' traffic complete on it, so each leg's traffic is counted per address from the address-channel handshake of its own transaction rather than from the responder's global transaction counters.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_dma_sanity_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DMA-OUTPUT-FABRIC-TRAFFIC` |Per leg and per address: exactly one read transaction at the source, one write transaction at the destination and one verification read at the destination reached the SYS_OUT responder. A transaction sent to the wrong address, or a second one at the same address, fails. The number of transactions other masters completed on the same responder is printed on the same line as reported, not asserted
|===


*Pass Criteria*: both copies match their expected destination words, the LENGTH = 1 leg leaves the destination's upper seven bytes at the poison, and each leg addressed the SYS_OUT responder exactly once per direction. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `coverage_closure`, `depth`.
'''''

[[sec-019-smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test]]
===== 19 `smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI); `tb_top.sv` sticky latches `tb_wdt_first_timeout_seen` / `tb_wdt_second_timeout_seen` on the `smc_wrapper` timeout outputs

*Objective*: A programmed first timeout raises `smc_wdt_first_timeout_o` at the DUT boundary; left uncleared, the stage-2 count in `CPU_CTRL.WDT_TIMEOUT` raises `smc_wdt_second_timeout_o`, and the reset unit turns that into the warm reset.

*Test Coverage*:

* core-0 WDT armed over SEP_IN with the magic-key protocol of `wdt.rdl` (`KEY` before each of `CMP`, `COUNT`, `CTRL`; `CTRL.wdogrsten | wdogenalways`);
* `smc_wdt_first_timeout_o` latched within a bound of arming, with `smc_wdt_second_timeout_o` still 0 on that cycle;
* `smc_wdt_second_timeout_o` latched `WDT_TIMEOUT` (`cpu_ctrl.rdl` reset `0x4000`) cycles after the first, within a stated slack;
* `rst_warm` still released on the cycle the first timeout latches, asserted after the second timeout, then released; both live pins read 0 afterwards while both sticky latches still read 1.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_wdt_timeout_pin_test_seq`. Each checker below is a bounded observation of a `tb_top` pin the DUT can fail to drive; a token is emitted only after that observation holds. `+smc_wdt_timeout_negative` inverts the second-timeout expectation (demands it on the cycle the first one latches) and must FAIL; it is the control for the second-timeout observer.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-FIRST` |`tb_wdt_first_timeout_seen` reaches 1 within `FIRST_TIMEOUT_BOUND_CYCLES` of the arming readback, `tb_wdt_second_timeout_seen` still 0 on that cycle
|`CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-SECOND` |`tb_wdt_second_timeout_seen` reaches 1 between `WDT_TIMEOUT` and `WDT_TIMEOUT + SECOND_TIMEOUT_SLACK_CYCLES` cycles after the first
|`CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-RESET` |`tb_rst_warm_smc_clk_n` reads 1 on the cycle the first timeout latches, falls within `WARM_RESET_BOUND_CYCLES` of the second timeout and rises again; live pins then 0, sticky latches 1
|===

*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-FIRST`, `CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-SECOND`, `CHK-WDT-TIMEOUT-RESET` hold after their observations. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`.
'''''

[[sec-184-smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test]]
===== 184 `smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (no external VIP); `tb_dma_busy` sampled on every `clk_smc_i` edge across the sweep

*Objective*: All sixteen DMA stream banks decode with `NUM_CTRL_STREAMS = 1`: the reserved `STATUS`, `DONE` and `NEXT_ID` registers complete OKAY and read the value `dma.adoc` gives a reserved bank, and no reserved `NEXT_ID` read launches a transfer.

*Test Coverage*:

* The zeros are given teeth: `DST_ADDRESS_LO` in the same block takes and returns a pattern before the sweep and is restored after it, so a bank reading 0 is reading from a block that answers rather than from a dead bus.
* `tb_dma_busy` is required resolvable and 0 at every sampled `clk_smc_i` edge across the whole sweep, and the watcher is required to have sampled at least one edge, so "no transfer started" is a per-sample compare and not an untaken branch.
* Bank 0 is the functional stream: its `STATUS_0` and `DONE_0` are hardware status the specification pins to no value, so they are read for an OKAY response only and their values are reported, not compared. `NEXT_ID_0` is not read here, because reading it launches a transfer.
* The DMA clock gate is saved, ungated for the sweep, and restored with an exact readback.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_dma_reserved_stream_banks_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer: ungate the DMA clock, start the busy watcher, write and read back the liveness pattern, read `STATUS_0` and `DONE_0` for a response, sweep `STATUS_1..15`, `DONE_1..15` and `NEXT_ID_1..15` against an exact 0, restore the pattern and the clock gate, then require the busy count to be 0 and the access count to have reached its floor. The leaf re-checks the scoreboard compare count and books a protocol-VIP record.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DMA-STREAM-BANKS` |The cell ledger: the `all-16-banks-decode` cell with the measured evidence that closed it -- the reserved sweep's exact zeros, bank 0's reported status, the liveness pattern and the sampled busy count. Entered only after those compares passed
|`CHK-DMA-RESERVED-STREAM-BANKS` |Summary line: banks decoded, SEP_IN accesses issued, and the busy-high count out of the sampled cycles. Logged after the busy compare and the stimulus floor have held
|`CHK-DMA-STREAM-BANKS-FLOOR` |The leaf requires the scoreboard's `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every reserved `STATUS`, `DONE` and `NEXT_ID` register reads exactly 0 over an OKAY response, the liveness pattern reads back exactly, `tb_dma_busy` is 0 at all sampled cycles, and the scoreboard books at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` compares. Bank 0's status values are reported only. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''


==== FABRIC

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequence for smc_axil_burst_idle_test.
* The eight idle SAMPLEs are a negative claim, so they run *after* a positive control: :func:`smc_axil_idle_test_seq.prove_axil_probe_alive` drives a real frontdoor SEP_IN AXI request that makes `tb_axil_any_master_active` (via its `tb_axil_efuse_bank_active` term) observe 1, and the test declares `probe_positive_controls = ("axil_external_active",)` for the third backable probe.
* Without those legs a stuck-at-0 / undriven / mis-tied probe passes all eight asserts identically (`[NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]`).
* `dtp_csr_active` is never exact-compared here: no positive control for it can exist in this TB (`tb_top.sv:1119` ties `axil_dtp_csr_resp = '0'`), so the shared :func:`smc_axil_idle_test_seq.assert_axil_idle` iterates `AXIL_CHECKABLE_FIELDS` and returns that field as OBSERVED-ONLY text.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_axil_burst_idle_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DIAG-AXIL-ACTIVE` |Positive control in `smc_diagnostic_vip_utils.py`: `tb_axil_any_master_active` samples 1 under the same eFuse-bank AXI-Lite request; the run fails if the OR never rises
|`CHK-EFUSE-BANK-AXIL-ACTIVE` |Positive control in `smc_efuse_vip_utils.py`, reached through `prove_axil_probe_alive`: `tb_axil_efuse_bank_active` samples 1 while a strict SEP_IN read of the eFuse-shim window (expected its generated reset) is in flight; the run fails if the probe never rises
|`CHK-NONVAC` |Non-vacuity count in `smc_axil_burst_idle_test_seq.py`
|`CHK-PROBE-AXIL-EXTERNAL-ALIVE` |Named compare in `smc_axil_burst_idle_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-DIAG-AXIL-ACTIVE`, `CHK-EFUSE-BANK-AXIL-ACTIVE`, `CHK-NONVAC`, `CHK-PROBE-AXIL-EXTERNAL-ALIVE` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `axil`, `per_ip`, `pyuvm`.
'''''

[[sec-024-smc_axil_idle_test]]

Without it a stuck-at-0 / undriven / mis-tied probe passes the idle assert identically (`[NEGATIVE-NEEDS-POSITIVE-CONTROL]`).
* **Idle leg** -- one `SmcAxilItem` SAMPLE through the observer agent, whose *backable* activity bits (`AXIL_CHECKABLE_FIELDS`) must all read exactly 0. `dtp_csr_active` is excluded: no positive control for it can exist in this TB, so it is reported OBSERVED-ONLY and never exact-compared (see :func:`assert_axil_idle`). The `CHK-AXIL-IDLE` evidence token is emitted only after both legs and the typed scoreboard-counter end gate passed (`[EVIDENCE-TOKEN-CONDITIONAL]`).

|`CHK-DIAG-AXIL-ACTIVE` |Positive control in `smc_diagnostic_vip_utils.py`, reached through `prove_axil_probe_alive`: `tb_axil_any_master_active` samples 1 under a real eFuse-bank AXI-Lite request; the run fails if the OR never rises
|`CHK-EFUSE-BANK-AXIL-ACTIVE` |Positive control in `smc_efuse_vip_utils.py`, reached through `prove_axil_probe_alive`: `tb_axil_efuse_bank_active` samples 1 while a strict SEP_IN read of the eFuse-shim window (expected its generated reset) is in flight; the run fails if the probe never rises
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-AXIL-IDLE`, `CHK-DIAG-AXIL-ACTIVE`, `CHK-EFUSE-BANK-AXIL-ACTIVE` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `axil`, `per_ip`, `pyuvm`.

[[sec-026-smc_deadspace_decode_test]]
===== 26 `smc_deadspace_decode_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_deadspace_decode_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Probe wrap-period offsets past PeakRDL SIZE and watch live CSRs.

*Test Coverage*:

* Prove whether SMC register windows alias unmapped offsets onto live CSRs.
* Golden legality is PeakRDL `SIZE` / `TOTAL_SIZE` read by symbol from `smc_addr.h` (`_block_extent` + `DeadspaceProbe.__post_init__`), never the xbar window and never a hand-copied literal.
* A dead write that wraps onto a live register is the defect under test.
* SCOPE -- OKAY-into-void: a write into unmapped space that returns OKAY and leaves the live register alone is tallied as ACCEPTED and is OUT OF SCOPE for eight of the nine probes; only `i2c0_intr_enable` carries `expect_refuse` and asserts on it, because the i2c_wrap SIZE range-check is a decode contract this testcase can hold the DUT to.
* For the other eight no authority establishes that the SMC xbar must DECERR an unmapped offset rather than silently accept it, so `accepted=N` in the summary is a reported tally and not a verdict.
* No document in the tree fixes that contract for the whole map; this testcase does not decide it.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_deadspace_decode_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|— |This leaf emits no `CHK-*` token. Fail paths are the sequence asserts.
|===


*Pass Criteria*: not a sign-off PASS. The leaf measures wrap-to-live aliasing (dead offsets in seven register windows wrap onto live registers with an OKAY response) and runs weekly in `rtl_issue` under `expect_fail`, with `expect_fail_match` pinned to the `wrap_to_live` / `read_alias` assertion; the observed failure grades PASS and an observed PASS grades `expected_fail_passed`. Fail paths are the sequence asserts. Known Limitations records the hole. Enrolled in: `rtl_issue`, `per_ip`.
'''''

[[sec-028-smc_filter_multi_entry_test]]
===== 28 `smc_filter_multi_entry_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_filter_multi_entry_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI)

*Objective*: Filter 16 inbound + 16 outbound CONFIG sweep.

*Test Coverage*:

* RTL exposes 16 inbound + 16 outbound filter entries; this sweep covers every one of the 32 slots rather than entry 0 of each direction.
* Each of the 32 slots is proven *individually discriminable*, not merely "a window that answers 0x3000": every slot gets a signature value unique to (direction, index), and the sweep is PHASED so that the 32 signatures are CO-RESIDENT when they are read back.
* Phasing is the whole point.
* A per-slot write/readback/restore cycle proves nothing about aliasing: only one slot is ever non-default at a time, so an RTL that mapped all 32 windows onto one physical register would return the value just written through the very same address and pass.
* Here phase 2 programs all 32 slots without restoring any of them, and phase 3 reads all 32 back while all 32 are still resident -- so a read of slot i can only return sig_i if slot i has storage of its own.
* Under total aliasing the register holds sig_31 when phase 3 starts and the very first readback fails; under any partial aliasing of two windows the later-written one overwrites the earlier and that slot's readback fails.
* Phase 1 checks the RDL reset content of every window before anything is written, and phases 4/5 restore and re-confirm the RDL default, so the run also carries reset-value evidence.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_filter_multi_entry_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-FILTER-MULTI-ENTRY-SLOT-IDENTITY` |Named compare in `smc_filter_multi_entry_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: `CHK-FILTER-MULTI-ENTRY-SLOT-IDENTITY` hold after their compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`.
'''''

[[sec-032-smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test]]
===== 32 `smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_jtag_vip

*Objective*: OKAY WR/RD, then the SYS_OUT responder's one-shot SLVERR and DECERR on both directions, each carried back to the manager unchanged, and an OKAY recovery on the same address.

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OSS U1-2/U1-3/U6-2: SYS_OUT error responses through the slave agent's one-shot faults.
* The outbound fabric is a conduit: an error the subordinate raises is the subordinate's verdict, and the fabric's obligation is to hand the requesting manager the same encoding, neither downgraded nor swallowed. Each injected code is observed on the SYS_OUT wire by the passive monitor and required of the manager, so a fabric that rewrote it fails.
* The response code is an exact expectation on each access, so a DECERR is not satisfied by a SLVERR.
* The errored accesses leave the stored word untouched, and the OKAY write/read pair that follows on the same address shows no error state was latched.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_output_fabric_slverr_inject_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SYS-OUT-ERROR-CODES` |Each injected response code -- read SLVERR, write SLVERR, write DECERR and read DECERR -- was counted exactly once on the SYS_OUT wire by the passive monitor and received by the JTAG-AXI manager with the same encoding, and the OKAY write/read pair that follows on the same address completed normally
|===


*Pass Criteria*: the OKAY path completes and is booked against the memory model, each injected error reaches the manager with the encoding it had on the SYS_OUT wire, the stored word is untouched by the errored accesses, and the following OKAY write/read pair completes normally. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `coverage_closure`, `depth`.
'''''

[[sec-035-smc_remap_cla_test]]
===== 35 `smc_remap_cla_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_remap_cla_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (AXI), ocah_jtag_vip

*Objective*: ALIAS_REMAP address translation, remap-table reset sweep, CLA window.

*Test Coverage*:

* **ALIAS_REMAP address translation** -- entry 0 is programmed (`REGION_START` / `REGION_END` / `REGION_ATTRS.offset|valid`) and a JTAG AXI write issued at the source address is observed **landing at the translated address** on the SYS_OUT responder (`tb_output_axi_last_addr`). The un-programmed identity case is the same-run positive control and the entry-disabled re-read is the same-run negative control, so "the remap moved the access" is distinguishable from "nothing happened".
* **SMC_MMODE_REMAP_0..7 / SMC_ALIAS_REMAP_0..7** -- per-entry decode *and* the RDL reset content (`expected=` on every read, not merely an OKAY response).
* **SMC_CLA_REG** -- the Cluster Local Aggregator window: an allow leg on real CLA registers (RDL reset values + a scratch write/readback) paired with the deny leg on unmapped in-window offsets, so the error response is attributable to *this* aperture's decode rather than to a dead bus.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_remap_cla_test_seq`. Each checker below is a named compare the DUT can fail; a token is emitted only after that compare holds.


*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CLA-RESET-SWEEP` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-CLA-WINDOW-ALLOW` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-CLA-WINDOW-DENY` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-IDENTITY` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-LANDING` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REMAP-ALIAS0-OFF` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|`CHK-REMAP-TABLE-RESET` |Named compare in `smc_remap_cla_test_seq.py`; the run fails if it mismatches
|===


*Pass Criteria*: not a sign-off PASS. The ALIAS_REMAP translation, the remap-table reset sweep and the CLA allow leg hold; the CLA deny leg fails because the first unmapped in-window offset, `+0x38` (0xC016_0038), completes with OKAY instead of SLVERR/DECERR. The leaf runs weekly in `rtl_issue` under `expect_fail` with `expect_fail_match` pinned to that `resp=0` assertion, so the recorded failure grades PASS and any other failure grades FAIL. Known Limitations records the hole. Enrolled in: `rtl_issue`, `per_ip`.
'''''

[[sec-179-smc_address_map_region_decode_test]]
===== 179 `smc_address_map_region_decode_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_address_map_region_decode_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (no external VIP); `tb_axil_external_active`, a mirror of `smc_external_req_o`, sampled on every `clk_smc_i` edge for the adopter external AXI-Lite window

*Objective*: Every address-map region an inbound SEP_IN master can reach decodes at a base-side register and at the last register of its extent; three addresses no block declares are answered DECERR by the fabric error slave; the external window is proven at its own AXI-Lite port.

*Test Coverage*:

* Every expectation comes from the generated register map (`hw/sys/smc/regs/gen/py/smc_reg.py`) or from a co-resident write/readback pattern, never from a first read of the DUT. A region whose reset is zero throughout is proven by the co-resident pattern rather than by a 0 == 0 compare, because a dead or unmapped window commonly reads 0.
* The co-resident shape writes several addresses before any of them is read back, so a decode that collapses two windows onto one physical register returns a neighbour's pattern and fails the exact compare -- which a per-address write-then-read cannot see.
* Three addresses no generated-map block declares and no crossbar rule covers (past the watchdog cluster, past the DFX block, past the fabric-control block) are declared expected-error to the SEP_IN monitor and required to answer DECERR from the fabric error slave.
* The APB4-fronted blocks are read at their 32-bit generated-map addresses, and 32-bit reads are required to return values with bits above 16 set, so the APB4 data path is exercised beyond a 16-bit window.
* The regions SEP_IN cannot reach in this bench are named in the log and left open rather than claimed: the PLIC and CLINT/BEU windows fold onto the local base in `smc_local_fabric`, the generated map declares no register in the debug-module region, and the M-mode/Xvisor remap regions are outbound apertures with no inbound decode pinned by the specification.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_address_map_region_decode_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer. The sequence sweeps every reachable region at its base side and at the top of its extent, probes the three beyond-region addresses, routes one access into the external window, requires the scoreboard's exact-value compare count to have advanced by its floor, and prints the cell ledger. The leaf then re-checks the scoreboard compare count and books a protocol-VIP record with the sequence's measured access count against a stimulus floor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ADDRESS-MAP-REGION` |The cell ledger. One line per coverage cell the sequence closed, naming the measured evidence that closed it, plus one line per cell left NOT closed in this bench with the reason. A cell is entered only after its own compare passed, and the ledger raises if no cell closed at all; the NOT-closed lines are recorded gaps, not compares
|`CHK-ADDRESS-MAP-REGION-DECODE` |Summary line: cells closed, the scoreboard exact-value compare count against its floor, the number of beyond-region DECERR probes, the external-port active cycles and the number of cells left open. Logged only once every per-region compare and the compare-count floor have held; it carries no compare of its own
|`CHK-ADDRESS-MAP-REGION-FLOOR` |The leaf requires the scoreboard's `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged, so a run whose stimulus stopped reaching the DUT fails instead of passing with a short sweep
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every region's generated-reset compare or co-resident readback holds, the three beyond-region addresses each answer DECERR, the external window access drives `smc_external_req_o`, and the scoreboard books at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` exact-value compares. The cells named NOT closed are not part of the claim. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-180-smc_address_window_top_decode_test]]
===== 180 `smc_address_window_top_decode_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_address_window_top_decode_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (no external VIP)

*Objective*: The last word of every SEP_IN-reachable window `memmap.adoc` pins answers, and answers from its own block: a window sized short bus-errors there and one sized long is answered by the next block.

*Test Coverage*:

* Scratchpad memory: the first and the last 64-bit word of the 128 KiB window hold distinct co-resident patterns and each reads back exactly, so the window reaches its last word without aliasing.
* Boot ROM: the last word of the 128 KiB region answers, returns neither resident scratchpad pattern, refuses a write with an error response, and still holds the same word afterwards -- which is what read-only means at this boundary.
* DMA controller and memory zeroer: with distinct patterns resident in `DMA_CTRL.DST_ADDRESS_LO` and `ZEROER_CTRL.DEST_ADDR`, the last word of each 512 B aperture answers and returns neither pattern.
* DMA stream banks: `NEXT_ID_1` through `NEXT_ID_15` read exactly 0 as `dma.adoc` states, each checked to sit at the 8-byte stream-bank stride from `NEXT_ID_0`. `NEXT_ID_0` is read last and for an OKAY response only, because reading it is the one access that launches a transfer.
* CLA: the trace-sink `TRDSTRAMIMPL` identification word, in the first 8 bytes of the window, reads its generated reset.
* The two window tops the chapter pins that the reference RTL sizes shorter -- the miscellaneous wrapper and the CLA -- are read with the error response tolerated and left open with their measured response and the chapter line they disagree with. They are recorded gaps, not claims.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_address_window_top_decode_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer, then let the leaf re-check the scoreboard's exact-value compare count and book a protocol-VIP record against a stimulus floor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ADDRESS-WINDOW-TOP` |The cell ledger: one line per window top closed with the measured evidence, plus one line per top left NOT closed with the response it actually gave and the chapter line it disagrees with. Cells are entered only after their compare passed; the NOT-closed lines are recorded gaps
|`CHK-ADDRESS-WINDOW-TOP-DECODE` |Summary line: window tops closed, the scoreboard exact-value compare count against its floor, the access count, and how many tops the reference RTL does not answer. Logged after the per-window compares and the floor have held
|`CHK-ADDRESS-WINDOW-TOP-FLOOR` |The leaf requires `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every closed window top matched its generated reset or its co-resident pattern exactly, the ROM write was refused and left the word unchanged, and the scoreboard booked at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` compares. The two short window tops are reported, not claimed. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-181-smc_alias_remap_spare_block_decode_test]]
===== 181 `smc_alias_remap_spare_block_decode_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_alias_remap_spare_block_decode_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (no external VIP)

*Objective*: `LOCAL_BASE` reaches the SMC's own resources through the alias remap's reset setting, and the ends of the eight-entry alias-remap array, the two spare register blocks and the four end mailbox halves each decode as separate storage.

*Test Coverage*:

* The first access of the run is a read at `LOCAL_BASE` itself -- the core-0 watchdog `CTRL` sits at window offset 0 -- taken before anything is written to the remap block, so the same access carries both the local-alias claim and the transparent-at-reset claim. `CMP` in the same block carries a non-zero generated reset, which is what makes the offset-0 read decode evidence rather than a 0 == 0 compare.
* Alias-remap regions 0 and 7, the ends of the array, are checked to sit at the chapter's 0x20 stride, configured with distinct co-resident `REGION_START` values and restored to the generated reset, so a decoder that collapses the eight instances onto one fails the readback.
* Spare blocks: the scratch word holds an all-ones pattern and reads it back exactly, so all 32 bits are storage; the chip-config block is written and must still read its generated reset, because every `chip_config.rdl` field is `sw = r`.
* Mailbox: the two halves of pair 0 and of pair 31 hold four distinct co-resident `IRQEN` patterns at once, then read back 0. The offset of each half inside a pair comes from the generated map.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_alias_remap_spare_block_decode_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer, then let the leaf re-check the scoreboard's exact-value compare count and book a protocol-VIP record against a stimulus floor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ALIAS-REMAP-SPARE-BLOCK` |The cell ledger: one line per cell closed with the measured evidence behind it. A cell is entered only after its compare passed, and the ledger raises if no cell closed
|`CHK-ALIAS-REMAP-SPARE-BLOCK-DECODE` |Summary line: cells closed, accesses issued and the scoreboard exact-value compare count against its floor, naming the `LOCAL_BASE` read taken before any remap write, the two alias-array ends, the two spare blocks and the four mailbox halves. Logged after those compares and the floor have held
|`CHK-ALIAS-REMAP-SPARE-BLOCK-FLOOR` |The leaf requires `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged
|===

*Pass Criteria*: the `LOCAL_BASE` and `CMP` reads match the generated resets, every co-resident pattern reads back exactly and every register is restored, the chip-config write leaves the register at its reset, and the scoreboard books at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` compares. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-185-smc_dual_base_addressing_test]]
===== 185 `smc_dual_base_addressing_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_dual_base_addressing_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (no external VIP)

*Objective*: `LOCAL_BASE` is fixed read-only at 0xC000_0000 and keeps that value across a write attempt, `GLOBAL_BASE` in the same block takes and returns a pattern through the identical write path, and `REGION_SIZE` reads its specified 16 MiB reset.

*Test Coverage*:

* The three expectations are the sentences of `memmap.adoc` (Memory Map); nothing is read back from the DUT to form them.
* The write-has-no-effect leg has a positive control in the same register block: `GLOBAL_BASE`, the programmable sibling, is written and read back through the same path, so a dropped `LOCAL_BASE` write is attributable to the read-only attribute and not to a dead write channel. The control is checked not to already hold the probe pattern, and `GLOBAL_BASE` is restored to the value read at entry.
* `LOCAL_BASE` is written last, so a DUT that wrongly accepted the write fails its own readback rather than silently re-basing every later access of the run.
* `smc_region_size_o` is unconnected in `tb_top`, so the pin-side effect of `REGION_SIZE` is not claimed here.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_dual_base_addressing_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer: read `REGION_SIZE` and `LOCAL_BASE` against their specified values, save `GLOBAL_BASE` and prove the write path on it, then attempt the `LOCAL_BASE` write and require the register to still read 0xC000_0000. The leaf re-checks the scoreboard compare count and books a protocol-VIP record against a stimulus floor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DUAL-BASE` |The cell ledger: `local-base-reads-c0000000` and `local-base-write-has-no-effect`, each with the measured evidence behind it, including the `GLOBAL_BASE` write that serves as the positive control
|`CHK-DUAL-BASE-ADDRESSING` |Summary line: the `LOCAL_BASE` value across the write attempt, the `GLOBAL_BASE` save/pattern/restore sequence and the `REGION_SIZE` reset. Logged after those compares and the stimulus floor have held
|`CHK-DUAL-BASE-FLOOR` |The leaf requires the scoreboard's `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged
|===

*Pass Criteria*: `REGION_SIZE` reads the specified 16 MiB reset, `LOCAL_BASE` reads 0xC000_0000 before and after the write attempt, `GLOBAL_BASE` returns the probe pattern and then the restored value, and the scoreboard books at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` compares. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-188-smc_peripheral_instance_decode_test]]
===== 188 `smc_peripheral_instance_decode_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_peripheral_instance_decode_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver; `tb_axil_external_active` sampled on every `clk_smc_i` edge for the PVT wrapper access, which the bench PVT model answers OKAY with zero data

*Objective*: Every peripheral block the generated map declares reads a register with a non-zero generated reset, every multi-instance peripheral is proven at its first and last instance, and the PVT wrapper is proven at the adopter external port.

*Test Coverage*:

* Single-instance blocks are closed by a register whose generated reset is non-zero, so the compare can tell a decoded block from an absent window that reads 0.
* Multi-instance peripherals are additionally proven with distinct patterns written to all probed instances before any is read back, so a stride that aliases two instances returns a neighbour's pattern and fails: the I3C CSR windows, the GPIO interfaces, the mailbox pairs, the watchdog instances and the log engines are each driven at their first and last instance.
* The PVT wrapper is proven at its consumer: the access must drive `smc_external_req_o` while in flight, not merely complete.
* Left open and named, not claimed: the DTP control window (`tb_top` leaves its AXI-Lite response idle, so an access there would never complete) and the bus error units (behind the bit-25 fold of `smc_local_fabric`, so no SEP_IN access reaches one).

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_peripheral_instance_decode_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer: sweep the I3C, GPIO, mailbox, watchdog and log-engine instances, then the single-instance blocks, then route the PVT access; name the two unreachable cells, require the exact-value compare count and the access count to have reached their floors, and print the cell ledger. The leaf re-checks the scoreboard compare count and books a protocol-VIP record.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-PERIPHERAL-INSTANCE` |The cell ledger: one line per block or instance cell closed with the measured evidence behind it, plus one line per cell left NOT closed in this bench with the tie-off or address fold that prevents it. The NOT-closed lines are recorded gaps, not compares
|`CHK-PERIPHERAL-INSTANCE-DECODE` |Summary line: cells closed, the scoreboard exact-value compare count against its floor, the access count, the PVT external-port active cycles and the number of cells left open. Logged after the per-block compares and both floors have held
|`CHK-PERIPHERAL-INSTANCE-FLOOR` |The leaf requires the scoreboard's `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every probed block matches its non-zero generated reset, every probed instance returns its own co-resident pattern, the PVT access drives the external port, and the scoreboard books at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` compares. The DTP window and the bus error units are recorded as not closed. Enrolled in no scheduled group. Held out of `all` and `hosted` on #1989 (the shadow map reads 0 after sense); the row is in `hw/sys/smc/doc/dv/SMC_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`.

'''''

[[sec-190-smc_region_size_plic_clint_beu_decode_test]]
===== 190 `smc_region_size_plic_clint_beu_decode_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_region_size_plic_clint_beu_decode_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (no external VIP)

*Objective*: At the 16 MiB `REGION_SIZE` reset the PLIC offset folds onto the bottom of the local window; with `REGION_SIZE` programmed to 256 MiB the PLIC, the CLINT and the four bus error units decode at the addresses the chapters give them.

*Test Coverage*:

* The fold is measured rather than assumed: at the reset aperture the PLIC priority word at window offset 0x20 returns the watchdog `CMP` reset, and once the aperture is widened the same address no longer returns it. Both halves are exact compares.
* 256 MiB is the smallest legal power of two whose aperture contains the whole documented map up to BASE + 0x801_3FFF; the sequence asserts that relation against the generated reset size rather than hard-coding a verdict.
* PLIC: the priority and per-core enable words hold distinct co-resident patterns, are then written to the priority-0 / disabled state `interrupts.adoc` requires of firmware and read back exactly; the top word of the 4 MiB window and the first word above it must answer without returning either pattern.
* CLINT: `MSIP_0` and `MTIMECMP_0` hold distinct co-resident patterns, and the top word of the 64 KiB window must not return them.
* BEU: the four `ENABLE` registers read their generated reset, are each checked against the specified 0x1000 stride, and then hold four distinct co-resident patterns, so the per-core instances are proven separate registers rather than one alias.
* `REGION_SIZE` is restored to its generated reset and a local-window register is read afterwards, so the aperture is left as the rest of the suite expects.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_region_size_plic_clint_beu_decode_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer: measure the fold at the reset aperture, widen `REGION_SIZE` and show the same address no longer folds, drive the PLIC, its window edges, the CLINT and the four BEUs, restore `REGION_SIZE` and re-read a local-window register, then require the exact-value compare floor and print the cell ledger. The leaf re-checks the scoreboard compare count and books a protocol-VIP record.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-REGION-SIZE-PLIC-CLINT-BEU` |The cell ledger: one line per cell closed with the measured evidence -- the 16 MiB fold, the PLIC base, edge and firmware-init cells, the CLINT base and top, and the four BEU instances. A cell is entered only after its compare passed
|`CHK-REGION-SIZE-PLIC-CLINT-BEU-DECODE` |Summary line: the folded word at the reset aperture, the unfolded word at 256 MiB, cells closed, accesses issued and the scoreboard exact-value compare count against its floor. Logged after those compares and the floor have held
|`CHK-REGION-SIZE-PLIC-CLINT-BEU-FLOOR` |The leaf requires the scoreboard's `sys_axi_value_checks_seen` to have reached `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` before this token is logged
|===

*Pass Criteria*: the fold at the reset aperture and its absence at 256 MiB both hold as exact compares, every PLIC, CLINT and BEU co-resident pattern reads back exactly, the window-edge reads return neither resident pattern, `REGION_SIZE` is restored and the local-window register reads its reset afterwards, and the scoreboard books at least `EXPECTED_VALUE_CHECKS` compares. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-192-smc_sys_axi_in_port_test]]
===== 192 `smc_sys_axi_in_port_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_sys_axi_in_port_test`

*BFM / Model*: the SEP_IN AXI driver for the filter programming and the readbacks, plus the SYS_IN AXI agent (`sys_in_axi_agent`) driving `sys_axi_in_req_i`

*Objective*: Once inbound filter entry 0 admits, `sys_axi_in` carries a read of a register with a non-zero generated reset and a write whose value both ports read back.

*Test Coverage*:

* Inbound entry 0 is programmed over SEP_IN with the pass-all recipe the output-fabric tests use, its config word is read back exactly, and all three of its registers are restored to their generated resets with a further config readback at the end.
* The SYS_IN read compares against the generated reset of `CHIP_CONFIG.VERSION_LO`, which is non-zero, so the compare distinguishes a decoded read from a window that answers 0.
* The scratch word written over SYS_IN is read back over SEP_IN and then over SYS_IN, so the same storage is shown reachable from the system port and from SEP_IN; the scratch register is restored to 0 with a readback.
* The access counts are the measured ones, not declarations: the sequence requires its own SEP_IN count to equal exactly the expected number and requires the scoreboard's completed SYS_IN AXI count to equal exactly four.
* The pre-admit SYS_IN read is issued with the error response tolerated and its response is reported, not asserted: the specification leaves the reset state of the sixteen inbound entries to the chiplet integration, so no verdict is claimed on it.
* The inbound filter decides each direction from its own request, with block-by-default when nothing matches, so the write and read channels of the port are driven together: four interleaved passes go out as one outstanding group, SEP_IN seeds the read sources and reads the write targets back, and the filter is observed admitting both directions in the same cycle.
* Every inbound port of this bench carries an agent, so the "unused port tied idle" cell cannot be driven here.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_sys_axi_in_port_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer, with the SYS_IN accesses issued as one-shots on the SYS_IN sequencer: pre-admit read, program and read back entry 0, SYS_IN read of `VERSION_LO`, SYS_IN scratch write, SEP_IN and SYS_IN readbacks, the interleaved write/read group over `sys_axi_in` with its seeds, readbacks and restores under a clocked observer on the filter decision, restore the scratch word and the filter entry, then require both access counts exactly. The leaf requires both SYS_IN read values to have been captured and books a protocol-VIP record carrying the measured SEP_IN and SYS_IN counts against their floors.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SYS-AXI-IN-READ` |After entry 0 admitted the whole address range, a `sys_axi_in` read of `CHIP_CONFIG.VERSION_LO` returned its non-zero generated reset exactly. The pre-admit response at the same address is printed on the same line as reported, not asserted
|`CHK-SYS-AXI-IN-WRITE` |A `sys_axi_in` write of the scratch pattern was read back exactly over SEP_IN and over `sys_axi_in`, and the scoreboard had completed exactly the expected number of SYS_IN and SEP_IN accesses
|`CHK-SYS-AXI-IN-NOT-CLOSED` |A recorded gap, not a compare: the unused-port-tied-idle cell, with the reason -- every inbound port of this bench carries an agent and none is tied to zero. Logged unconditionally
|`CHK-SYS-AXI-IN-PORT` |Ledger line in the leaf: the SYS_IN read value, the scratch readback and the SEP_IN access count, logged after the leaf requires both SYS_IN reads to have been captured. The compares are the sequence's and the scoreboard's
|`CHK-SYS-AXI-IN-DUPLEX` |The interleaved write/read group over `sys_axi_in`: every read returned the word SEP_IN seeded and the write targets held the last pass, and the inbound filter was observed admitting both directions in at least one cycle -- both filter hits set with neither isolate -- so a run whose two channels never overlapped fails
|===

*Pass Criteria*: the admitted `sys_axi_in` read matches the generated reset, the scratch write reads back exactly over both ports, the filter entry and the scratch register are restored, and the measured SEP_IN and SYS_IN access counts equal the expected ones. The pre-admit response and the tied-idle cell are recorded, not claimed. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''


[[sec-194-smc_sep_in_axi_shape_test]]
===== 194 `smc_sep_in_axi_shape_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_sep_in_axi_shape_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (`ocah_axi_vip` AXI4 master), no external VIP

*Objective*: The transfer shapes the 64-to-32-bit conversion chain on the SEP_IN path has to resolve: narrow AxSIZE with byte strobes, a multi-beat INCR burst, and the FIXED and WRAP burst types the data-width converter rejects.

*Test Coverage*:

* AxSIZE below the bus width is a narrow transfer whose byte strobes select the lanes. A one-byte write at offset 0 and a two-byte write at offset 2 must each leave the rest of the word alone, so the readback is the merge of the three writes and not any one of them.
* Narrow reads return the addressed lanes of the same word, so the strobe placement is shown on both directions.
* An AxLEN=1 INCR burst is split into consecutive single-beat accesses: the two beats land in two consecutive scratch registers, are read back individually and again as one two-beat burst read, so a beat dropped, merged or reordered fails an exact compare.
* FIXED and WRAP multi-beat bursts are not supported by the data-width converter, which raises `aw_throw_error` for both and answers through its `axi_err_slv` with SLVERR. The response code is an exact expectation, and both target registers must still hold the values the INCR burst left, so a converter that errored the response while still writing is caught.
* Both scratch registers are restored to zero before the sequence ends.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_sep_in_axi_shape_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer: seed a scratch word, overwrite one byte and then one half of it with narrow writes and read the merge back, read the same lanes narrowly, place a two-beat INCR burst across two scratch registers and read it back per register and as one burst, issue the FIXED and the WRAP burst and require SLVERR with both registers unchanged, then restore both registers. The leaf requires the scoreboard's exact-value compare count to have reached its floor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SEP-IN-NARROW-STROBES` |The seeded word, the word after the one-byte write and the word after the two-byte write are exact scoreboard compares, as are the narrow reads of the same lanes; a strobe the fabric ignored or widened fails one of them
|`CHK-SEP-IN-BURST-INCR` |The two beats of the AxLEN=1 INCR write are read back from their own registers and again as one AxLEN=1 read, all exact compares
|`CHK-SEP-IN-BURST-UNSUPPORTED` |The FIXED and the WRAP burst each answered SLVERR exactly -- the scoreboard holds the access to that code, so another error code fails -- and both registers still read the values the INCR burst left
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every narrow and burst readback matches its exact expectation, the two rejected burst types answer SLVERR without changing either register, and the scoreboard books at least the expected number of value compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-195-smc_sep_in_axi_flow_control_test]]
===== 195 `smc_sep_in_axi_flow_control_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_sep_in_axi_flow_control_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (`ocah_axi_vip` AXI4 master) with a per-access `AxiTimingProfile` holding the response channels back

*Objective*: With several SEP_IN transactions outstanding and the manager holding BREADY and RREADY low, every access is still answered with the data of the address it was issued for, and the design keeps its response channels stable across the wait.

*Test Coverage*:

* AMBA IHI 0022 A3.2.1: a transfer completes only when VALID and READY are both asserted, VALID may not be withdrawn before that, and the channel's payload stays stable across the wait. B and R are driven by the design, so their stability under the manager's backpressure is the design's obligation and is sampled every cycle the manager holds READY low.
* Three write passes over the eight scratch registers go out as one outstanding group with BREADY held, each pass carrying its own word, so the individual readbacks also show that writes to one address took effect in the order they were issued.
* Four read passes over the same registers go out as one outstanding group with RREADY held, each compared against that address's word, so an access that was dropped, merged, reordered or mis-routed returns the wrong word.
* The stalled-cycle counts are asserted non-zero for all four channels. They are the measurement that the backpressure happened, not the claim: a run whose timing profile stopped reaching the bus fails rather than passing on traffic that never stalled.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_sep_in_axi_flow_control_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer with a clocked observer on the SEP_IN handshake lines: issue the write group under a BREADY hold, read each register back individually, issue the read group under an RREADY hold, restore the registers, then require the observer's violation list to be empty and every channel to have stalled at least once. The leaf requires the scoreboard's exact-value compare count to have reached its floor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SEP-IN-FLOW-CONTROL` |Every outstanding write and read completed with the word of the address it named -- the individual readbacks show the last write pass to each address, the read group repeats the same expectation from deeper in the pipeline -- and every one of the four channels was observed stalled at least once
|`CHK-SEP-IN-RESP-STABLE` |Across every cycle in which the manager held BREADY or RREADY low, BVALID and RVALID stayed asserted and BRESP/BID and RDATA/RRESP/RLAST/RID did not change. A withdrawn VALID or a changed payload is recorded and fails the run
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every access in both outstanding groups returns its own address's word, no response channel withdrew VALID or moved its payload while READY was low, all four channels stalled at least once, and the scoreboard books at least the expected number of value compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-196-smc_axil_downstream_concurrency_test]]
===== 196 `smc_axil_downstream_concurrency_test`

*Source Module*: `smc_axil_downstream_concurrency_test`

*BFM / Model*: SEP_IN AXI driver (`ocah_axi_vip` AXI4 master); the eFuse-bank window is backed by the eFuse-bank model and the mandatory external window by the reference integration's AXI-Lite error slave

*Objective*: The three downstream AXI-Lite manager ports are independent branches: with accesses to two of the windows outstanding at once, both ports are active in the same cycle and each access is answered by its own window.

*Test Coverage*:

* The two windows answer differently in both response code and data -- the eFuse shim register with OKAY and its generated reset, the mandatory external window with the terminator's DECERR and zero data -- so a crossed route fails on the response code and on the data rather than only on a count.
* The eFuse half is compared by the scoreboard against the generated reset; the external half is required to carry the terminator's zero data, which a neighbouring responder answering in its place would not.
* The cycles in which two of the three activity probes were high together are counted and required to be non-zero, so a group the fabric serialised after all fails instead of passing.
* The SEP_IN monitor attributes a response beat to the last address it saw accepted, which cannot name the transaction while two reads are outstanding; both addresses of the group are registered with it for that reason, and the per-access verdict is the scoreboard's exact expected response code.

*Test Procedure*:

Drive `smc_axil_downstream_concurrency_test_seq` on the SEP_IN AXI sequencer with a clocked observer on the three downstream activity probes: issue sixteen eFuse-shim reads alternated with sixteen external-window reads as one outstanding group, then require each external read to carry zero data, each eFuse read to have answered OKAY, and at least one cycle with two ports active together. The leaf requires the scoreboard's exact-value compare count to have reached its floor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-AXIL-DOWNSTREAM-CONCURRENCY` |Every eFuse-shim read answered OKAY with the generated reset and every external-window read answered DECERR with zero data while both accesses were outstanding together, and two downstream manager ports were observed active in the same cycle at least once. The per-probe active-cycle counts are printed on the same line as reported, not asserted
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every access is answered by its own window with that window's response code and data, at least one cycle had two downstream manager ports active together, and the scoreboard books at least the expected number of value compares. Enrolled in: `all`, `axil`, `hosted`, `per_ip`, `pyuvm`.

'''''


==== RESET
